"But seeing the kind of girl he's pouring his heart out for... seeing how good your heart is? I think he's got the best taste in the whole universe."

Emilia's face erupted in a brilliant shade of red. "I--- I'm--- Thank you! I mean, I'm just---"

"He's right," Naoko interrupted softly, leaning closer. "Thank you, Emilia-chan. For wanting families to be together."

Emilia looked between the two parents, the people who had lost their son to her world. The weight of their gratitude, combined with the acceptance on the screen, finally broke her composure. She hid her face behind her hands, a few happy tears escaping her amethyst eyes as she nodded fiercely.

After leaving the building, he heard Ram's voice from behind him.

"Emilia-sama...seems to have changed a bit. Is it because of something you said to her?" Ram crossed her arms and smiled warmly at him.

In the theater, Rem blinked. She rubbed her eyes with the back of her hands and stared at the screen as if it had suddenly grown a second head. "Nee-sama is... smiling? At Subaru-kun? Without hiding an insult in it?"

"I must be hallucinating," Otto whispered from his seat, leaning forward so much he nearly fell out of his chair.

"The world's endin', bro," Garfiel muttered, his jaw practically hitting the floor as he stared at the pink-haired maid on the screen. "Ram's actually bein' nice to th' Cap'n."

In the back row, Ram stiffened. Her face flushed a very faint shade of pink, and she defensively crossed her arms in an exact mirror of her on-screen counterpart. "It is merely a trick of the lighting. Barusu is incapable of drawing out a genuine smile from Ram."

"Oh? Is that so, my d~eee~ar Ram?" Roswaal chuckled beside her, his mismatched eyes gleaming with immense amusement. "I suppose the 'trick of the lighting' also altered the tone of your vo~iii~ce?"

Ram merely clicked her tongue and looked away, refusing to dignify her master's teasing with a response.

"It's the result of her thinking things through on her own." He replied warmly. "You shouldn't assume all her decisions come from someone else."

Emilia lowered her hands from her face, the blush still dusting her cheeks, but a profound sense of gratitude replaced her embarrassment. Subaru wasn't taking credit for her growth. Even behind her back, he was defending her autonomy and her strength.

"He's grown into a remarkably mature young man," Wilhelm observed softly, nodding in quiet approval. "To guide someone, yet refuse to claim ownership of their success... it is the mark of a true knight."

Kenichi puffed out his chest just a fraction more, beaming with fatherly pride.

But it was the next exchange that truly shattered the theater's composure.

"You're right. I was in the wrong this time." Ram said guiltily.

Dead silence reigned in the room for three entire seconds.

"HUH?!" Felt yelled, breaking the quiet. "Did the pink maid just apologize to him?!"

"She admitted she was wrong!" Tivey gasped, adjusting his monocle as if the glass had deceived him. "To Natsuki-san!"

"Nee-sama..." Rem whispered, looking back at her sister with an expression of pure, unadulterated awe. "You actually apologized to Subaru-kun..."

Ram's face was now burning a bright, undeniable red. She gripped her own elbows tightly, glaring daggers at the back of everyone's heads. "Shut your mouths, all of you! It was a momentary lapse in judgment! Barusu clearly caught me when I was fatigued from dealing with his usual nonsense!"

"Sure he did, Ram-san," Otto snickered, unable to help himself despite the lethal glare he was receiving. "We completely believe you."

End credits: Memento

The scene changed to sprouting fires, put on lamps to light up the night sky. The grounds of the ruin that was at the center of the Sanctuary were shown, with Emilia standing before it and a bunch of glowing orbs lighting around her.

"Woah... those are pretty." Kenichi commented with a look of awe. Naoko kept his jaw closed by tapping his chin lightly.

"Did you get enough support from the lesser spirits?" Subaru asked her, Otto on the carriage beside him with Garfiel and Ram looking on from the sidelines.

"Yeah. Everything's fine." She turns around with a small smile. "Still, I think I'd like one last cheer."

"Oho!" Kenichi grinned, leaning forward in his seat and rubbing his hands together. "She's asking for it! Get ready, Naoko. He's about to do the thing."

"The thing?" Crusch asked, arching an elegant eyebrow at the Natsuki patriarch.

"You'll see," Naoko giggled behind her hand, her eyes twinkling with amusement.

"Can I do it?" Natsuki asked enthusiastically.

"I want you to. Please." Emilia gave him a knowing smile.

Julius, who had been expecting a standard knightly vow of encouragement, blinked in confusion at the sheer excitement radiating from Subaru. "He looks as though he's been waiting his entire life for this specific request."

He hadn't seen anything yet.

"You can do it! Don't give up! EMC! Emilia-tan, major challenger!" He shouted, bringing his all into it so his body could shape like the letters in an amazing form of stupidity only available to the autistic--- I mean artistic.

Felt burst out laughing so hard she almost choked on her own spit. "PFFT- AHAHAHA! What the hell is that?!"

"Such unorthodox poses..." Reinhard murmured, looking mildly impressed despite himself. "I don't think I could contort my body with such speed without utilizing my Divine Protection of Evasion."

"That ain't an evasion technique, Sword Saint, it's just plain stupid," Garfiel snorted, though he had a wide, toothy grin on his face. "Cap'n sure knows how to make a fool of 'imself to make 'er smile."

Julius rubbed his temples, letting out a long, suffering sigh. "I take back my previous statement about his maturity. The man has the dignity of a court jester."

"Oh, hush, Julius-san," Anastasia giggled from her seat, thoroughly entertained. "I think it's sweet! Ain't many men willin' to look that foolish just to give their lady a boost."

"Truly, a display of ultimate foolishness, I suppose," Beatrice sighed, crossing her arms over her chest, though there was an undeniable fondness in her eyes. "Betty's Subaru is hopeless, in fact."

Kenichi, meanwhile, had thrown a fist into the air, mirroring his son's energy. "That's my boy! You gotta put your whole back into it if you want the girl to notice!"

"Thank you." Emilia said, her smile bright and completely devoid of any embarrassment at his antics.

In the theater, Emilia hid the lower half of her face behind her silver hair, though her amethyst eyes were crinkled in happiness. Even with an audience judging him, his silly cheers still worked perfectly to warm her heart.

After that, Emilia headed for the entrance to the tomb, which began to glow as she approached.

"That means she's been approved as a qualified challenger in the Trial," Garfiel explained from the side, knowing Subaru was confused.

"I'm going now." The girl said confidently.

"She looks so brave," Naoko whispered, squeezing her husband's hand. "I hope it isn't too scary inside."

Emilia, in her seat, subconsciously pulled her knees closer together. She knew exactly how terrifying the inside of that tomb was. The memories of the snow, the blood, and the isolation were still too fresh in her mind.

Garfiel was looking at her retreating with a frown, which was noticed by Subaru.

"I get that it sucks being trapped here." Subaru gained Garfiel's attention as he hardened his eyes on him. "But why didn't you do this yourself?"

"If I could, I would, damn it." The blond man grumbled with a sharp-toothed snarl. Ram gave him a forlorn expression as Subaru raised an eyebrow.

In the theater, Garfiel clicked his tongue and sank lower into his seat, crossing his arms defensively over his chest. He actively avoided making eye contact with anyone, especially Frederica, who was looking at him with a mixture of sorrow and understanding.

"It seems there is much more to Garfiel-dono's reluctance than simple stubbornness," Wilhelm observed, his sharp eyes catching Ram's uncharacteristic display of pity. "A past attempt, perhaps? One that ended poorly?"

"Everyone in that Sanctuary's got a mountain of baggage," Al muttered from the back, leaning his helmet against the wall.

To their surprise, the glow of the tomb suddenly dried up.

"H-Hey, is she gonna be okay?" Subaru asked.

"The light's not supposed to go out as long as the trial's going on..." Garfiel looked at the grounds with uncertainty.

"What does that mean?!" Kenichi bolted upright in his chair, his paternal worry instantly shifting from his son to the girl his son loved. "Did something happen to her in there?"

"The trial was rejected?" Julius leaned forward, his brow furrowed in deep concern. "Or perhaps an external force interfered?"

"Emilia-sama..." Rem whispered, looking toward the trembling half-elf.

Emilia sat completely frozen in the theater. She wrapped her arms around herself as if staving off a sudden, biting chill. The lights going out... she remembered exactly what happened next. The crushing weight of her forgotten past, the paralyzing fear, and the agonizing feeling of being utterly, completely alone in the dark.

"I..." Emilia forced the word out of her trembling lips, refusing to look at the screen. "I failed... It was so cold..."

Seeing her distress, Naoko didn't hesitate. She leaned across the small gap between their seats and gently placed a warm hand over Emilia's shivering ones. "It's alright, sweetie," Naoko said softly, her motherly tone cutting through the rising tension in the room. "You're right here. You're safe."

Emilia let out a shaky breath, grounding herself in the warmth of Naoko's touch, even as the screen promised to show everyone her darkest moments.

"Emilia!" Natsuki rushed into the tomb.

"Barusu!" Ram tried to stop him, worriedly.

To everyone's even greater surprise, the building began to glow again as the young man approached.

"Why..." Ram said in disbelief.

"I don't understand, but if I can get in there, that's good enough for me! Just stay there! I'll yell if I need you!" Subaru shouted at them without stopping.

"Always jumping headfirst into the fire," Julius sighed, though there was an undeniable respect in his gaze. "A foolish knight, but a knight nonetheless."

"So that's what Echidna gave to him..." Emilia pursed her lip, hugging her knees tighter. "And he ran in after I had failed..."

He ran along the glowing corridor until he saw an open door ahead. He entered and found the lying unconscious Emilia.

"Emilia!"

But before he could reach her, his mind went blank, and a voice suspiciously similar to his own sounded in his head.

"First, you need to face your own past."

After that, consciousness left him to fall right beside Emilia.

*THUUUUUD *

The entire theater seemed to suck in a collective breath.

"His... past?" Kenichi breathed out, his voice suddenly hollow. He slumped back into his seat, the jovial energy he had carried just moments ago evaporating entirely.

Naoko's hand tightened around Kenichi's arm in a vise grip. Her eyes were wide, glued to the screen in a mixture of profound hope and paralyzing fear. "Are we... are we going to see..." She couldn't finish the sentence.

"His home," Al whispered from the back, his helmet shifting slightly as he leaned forward, entirely captivated. The single word hung in the air, heavy with the weight of another world.

Emilia and Rem both stared at the screen, their hearts pounding against their ribs. They were finally going to see it. The world that shaped him. The life he had lived before he became their hero, before the pain, before the looping. The boy named Natsuki Subaru, before Lugunica.

He woke up in bed... In a strange room darkened by curtains. There were various books on the shelves above his bed with small figurines of little characters in colorful, stylish outfits, frozen in time, doing complex poses to show off their figures.

"Good morning, son of mine!" The next moment, a muscular middle-aged man with short black hair, naked, well-trained torso, and in orange sweatpants fell on the Subaru.

Subaru groaned as the weight of the man settled onto him, all sleep gone from his eyes as his lungs battled for air.

"Hey, c'mon! What? What? What's wrong? You look as if you just saw your own dad naked first thing in the morning!" The newcomer stood tall, smiling down at Subaru with a shine on his teeth that resembled Garfiel's and Subaru's own throughout the entire viewing.

Subaru looked at him... dumbfounded.

Title card: The Long-Awaited Reunion

Season 2 Episode 3. End.

In the theater, the shock was instantaneous and absolutely deafening.

Nearly every single head in the room snapped so fast they risked whiplash, looking from the muscular, grinning man on the screen directly to the man sitting in the middle row holding his wife's hand.

Kenichi Natsuki sat utterly paralyzed. His mouth opened, but no sound came out. The jovial, confident patriarch of the Natsuki household was reduced to a trembling statue as he stared at his own face projected on the giant screen.

"Wait... wait a second!" Garfiel yelled, pointing a shaking finger at the screen and then at Kenichi. "That's... That's you! Th' exact same teeth 'n everythin'!"

"Natsuki-san?" Otto gasped, completely bewildered by the sudden appearance of the man sitting right next to him.

"It's... it's really him," Emilia whispered, her amethyst eyes darting between the screen and Kenichi. The realization hit her like a tidal wave. "Subaru... He... The same thing as my trial..."

"Oh, my baby..." Naoko finally broke. The dam she had been holding back since the very beginning of this nightmare shattered into a million pieces. She buried her face into Kenichi's shoulder, her shoulders heaving with violent, agonizing sobs. "He's home... look, Kenichi, he's in his bed. He's safe."

Kenichi's eyes immediately filled with heavy, unshed tears. He wrapped both arms around Naoko, burying his face in her hair as his own chest hitched uncontrollably. The sight of that normal, mundane morning---a morning he had taken for granted a thousand times over, a routine he thought would last forever---was a dagger straight to his heart.

It was the morning his son had disappeared. The last morning they had ever spent together.

"Yeah... yeah, he is," Kenichi choked out, his voice thick with a grief so profound it silenced the rest of the room. He looked up at the screen, tears freely streaming down his cheeks as he watched his on-screen self smile down at his boy.

"That's my boy," Kenichi wept, pulling his wife closer as the entire theater watched the Natsuki family reunite, if only in a memory. "That's my stupid, wonderful boy."

Rem covered her mouth with both hands, her own tears falling freely as she watched the Natsuki parents break down. She looked at the screen, at the dumbfounded Subaru, and then at the father who loved him so fiercely.

"Told ya..." Felt couldn't but grimace at the parents from her seat. "This will only get worse from 'ere."

"This prison and it's cruelty..." Crusch looked away from the pair so she can give them a little bit of respect as the screen above refused to let them rest.

"Sooner or later... You'll be able to talk with him all about it, Natsuki-san." Otto whispered over the two, as they held each other beside him. Having Garfiel nod his head firmly.

"As soon as he wakes up... I promise."

Roswaal's eccentric smile turned thin after hearing Otto's words. With his fingers intertwining.
